Redress and judgment do not merely end a dispute; cohorts enter a city-like domain of obedience and reckoning, turning verdict into durable social form.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ن ص ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001510","branch_id":"B002","role":"Redress after oppression supplies the justice claim behind the new order."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B003","role":"Adjudication supplies the decisive settlement from which an accountable order can begin."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"د خ ل","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000464","branch_id":"B001","role":"Entry into an interior makes the settled order something people inhabit."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"د ي ن","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000504","branch_id":"B002","role":"Accounting and recompense supply the order's answerability rather than mere allegiance."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"د ي ن","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000504","branch_id":"B006","role":"The city of obedience supplies a civic-spatial image for the domain entered."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"ف و ج","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001184","branch_id":"B001","role":"Human cohorts supply the population by which the verdict becomes social order."}],"structural_cues":["The result of 110:1 is followed by repeated plural entry rather than a description of a defeated opponent.","The container construction في دين allows a spatial-social model without requiring دين itself to be a literal city."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies redress, judgment, entry, accounting, and a city-of-obedience image; I infer that the verdict is institutionalized as an inhabitable order. Alternatively, city and accounting may remain separate lexical branches with no civic mechanism here.","changed_reading":{"before":"Divine judgment ends an oppressive dispute.","after":"The settlement opens an accountable order that groups can enter and inhabit, so justice becomes a social arrangement rather than a terminating verdict."}},{"model_id":"delta_opening_as_postvictory_beginning","status":"revised","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["س ب ح","ح م د","ر ب ب"],"mechanism":"The opening is not the end-state but the beginning that opens what follows: worship, a praiseworthy aim, nurture, completion, and growth. The conqueror's task changes from obtaining the event to being formed after it.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B002","role":"An opening beginning that unlocks what follows makes victory the first phase rather than the finish."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"س ب ح","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000666","branch_id":"B001","role":"Worship through glorification and prayer supplies the first discipline opened by the event."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ح م د","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000355","branch_id":"B004","role":"The praiseworthy end supplies a telos beyond simply obtaining victory."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B002","role":"Nurture, repair, and completion supply the formative work required after opening."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000537","branch_id":"B005","role":"The non-dominant split image of nourishment and growth extends completion into an exploratory organic process."}],"structural_cues":["The فـ at the start of 110:3 links imperatives to the achieved condition in the preceding ayat.","The sequence moves from public influx to second-person worship and self-correction."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies an opening-beginning, consequent worship, a praiseworthy end, nurture, and growth; I infer that victory inaugurates formation. A live alternative is that 110:3 prescribes gratitude after a fully complete event without redefining the opening.","changed_reading":{"before":"The opening is the terminal achievement for which one later gives thanks.","after":"The opening begins a post-victory formation in which worship, repair, maturation, and a praiseworthy end still have to unfold."}},{"model_id":"delta_antiboast_guard","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ر ء ي","س ب ح","ح م د","غ ف ر"],"mechanism":"A publicly visible opening carries a semantic shadow of display and self-credit. Glorification, praise, and seeking protective forgiveness function as a guard against converting divine aid into the victor's boast.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ء ل ه","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000047","branch_id":"B001","role":"The worshipped one fixes the proper object of devotion and attribution."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B009","role":"Boastful display of wealth or learning supplies the dangerous shadow carried by an achieved opening."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"ر ء ي","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000531","branch_id":"B005","role":"Showing oneself before people makes public visibility the setting in which self-display can arise."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"س ب ح","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000666","branch_id":"B002","role":"Declaring transcendence and clearing from fault redirects the event away from human self-magnification."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ح م د","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000355","branch_id":"B005","role":"Claiming praise by conferring a favor names the precise appropriation risk that praise of the Lord reverses."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"غ ف ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001096","branch_id":"B002","role":"Covering a fault and protecting its bearer from its effect supplies remediation for the victor's attributional failure."}],"structural_cues":["Public seeing and mass movement are followed immediately by commands centered on the Lord rather than the human witness.","Praise and seeking forgiveness are coordinated in the response to success."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies public display, a boast branch of opening, praise-claiming, transcendence, and forgiveness; I infer an ego hazard generated by success and a ritual guard against it. Alternatively, the commands may be generic devotion with no implied boast risk.","changed_reading":{"before":"The opening authorizes uncomplicated celebration by the victorious party.","after":"The opening is also an attributional test: its visibility can feed self-display, so its proper completion requires praise redirected to the Lord and protection from self-credit."}},{"model_id":"delta_open_then_protect","status":"revised","confidence":"exploratory","trigger_roots":["د خ ل","غ ف ر"],"mechanism":"فتح and غفر create a controlled-boundary paradox. The aperture must open for entry, yet opening also permits hidden corruption or an alien element to mingle; protective covering is therefore part of governing, not undoing, permeability.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B001","role":"A widened aperture supplies the necessary but potentially vulnerable boundary condition."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"د خ ل","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000464","branch_id":"B004","role":"Hidden corruption supplies a possible adverse interior effect of successful entry."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"د خ ل","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000464","branch_id":"B005","role":"An outsider who mingles with a group supplies the permeability risk without negating genuine entrants."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"غ ف ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001096","branch_id":"B001","role":"Covering that preserves and protects supplies the counter-operation that makes openness governable."}],"structural_cues":["Entry through the opened field precedes the command to seek covering and protection.","The sequence preserves both openness and a subsequent protective act rather than choosing one boundary state."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies aperture, entry, hidden interior damage, mingling, and protective covering; I infer that opening creates a need for governed protection. Alternatively, غفر may concern only moral fault and have no boundary-management role.","changed_reading":{"before":"Success is maximal, unqualified openness after a barrier falls.","after":"Success is selective permeability: the boundary opens for entry but must also be protected against harms that openness can carry inward."}},{"model_id":"delta_event_within_enduring_return","status":"revised","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ر ب ب","ك و ن","ت و ب"],"mechanism":"The punctual coming of 110:1 is nested within continuity in 110:3. The opening starts what follows, while enduring lordship and the solicitation of return prevent the event from closing history into a completed triumph.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ج ي ء","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000281","branch_id":"B001","role":"Arrival supplies the bounded event that occurs at a threshold in time."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B002","role":"An opening beginning supplies the event's forward-facing rather than terminal aspect."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B007","role":"Abiding, residence, and continuance supply durable lordship around the punctual event."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ك و ن","source_word_indices":["6"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001332","branch_id":"B001","role":"Occurrence and presence in time make the closing description explicitly temporal."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ت و ب","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000189","branch_id":"B003","role":"Calling another to repent supplies an ongoing invitation to return after the opening."}],"structural_cues":["The threshold construction إذا جاء is answered later by a clause containing كان.","The event nouns are followed by imperatives and a stable divine description rather than a narrative stop."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies a punctual arrival, an opening-beginning, continuance, temporal presence, and elicited return; I infer that the event is nested in an enduring return-process. Alternatively, كان may simply support a conventional divine predicate without contrasting temporal scales.","changed_reading":{"before":"Victory arrives once and closes the prior story.","after":"A punctual opening occurs inside enduring lordship and begins repeated reorientation; the event is real but not self-sufficient or terminal."}},{"model_id":"delta_social_irrigation","status":"strengthened","confidence":"exploratory","trigger_roots":["ف و ج","س ب ح","ر ب ب"],"mechanism":"The baseline water network acquires a social-ecological extension: rain and tributaries reach an opened channel, cohorts move through a broad gap like distributed flow, and the result is nurture and growth. The people are not equated with water; flow is the reader-supplied analogy linking the packet's material branches.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ج ي ء","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000281","branch_id":"B003","role":"The water-gathering hollow supplies the catchment into which distant relief converges."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ن ص ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001510","branch_id":"B004","role":"Relieving rain that causes growth supplies the life-giving input."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ن ص ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001510","branch_id":"B007","role":"Tributaries bringing water from afar supply convergence and distribution."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B005","role":"Water released through an opening supplies controlled outflow from the achieved threshold."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"ف و ج","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001184","branch_id":"B001","role":"Human groups supply the distributed social units carried by the analogy."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"ف و ج","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001184","branch_id":"B002","role":"A broad gap supplies the channel widened for grouped passage."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"س ب ح","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000666","branch_id":"B004","role":"Running or swimming supplies movement through a medium rather than static accumulation."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B008","role":"Rain-bearing cloud supplies an upstream ecological counterpart to the rain-relief branch."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000537","branch_id":"B005","role":"The split image of nourishment and growth supplies the downstream effect of distributed flow."}],"structural_cues":["The sequence moves from arrival and opening to grouped entry and then to an imperative whose root also carries free movement through water.","Plural cohorts prevent the material analogy from collapsing into one undifferentiated mass."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies rain, tributaries, an opened watercourse, a broad gap, grouped movement, cloud, and growth; I infer a social-irrigation analogy connecting them. A live alternative is accidental branch resonance with no intended cross-domain mechanism.","changed_reading":{"before":"The water branches offer a self-contained image of relief reaching an outlet.","after":"Exploratorily, the opening distributes divine relief into social growth: cohorts move through it as differentiated flows through a widened, life-giving channel."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"outlier_victory_becomes_report","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it uses the non-dominant ر و ي mapping behind رأيت rather than the dominant sight branch. It remains anchored to the arrived event and to the packet's explicit split inventory, but downstream prose should call it a transmission afterimage, not translate رأيت as narration.","focus_anchor":"An event that comes and opens can pass from occurrence into a carried account of that occurrence.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ج ي ء","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000281","branch_id":"B001","role":"Arrival supplies the event that can subsequently become reportable."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ن ص ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001510","branch_id":"B001","role":"Prevailing aid supplies the event-content carried into public memory."},{"source_ref":"110:2","root":"ر ء ي","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000615","branch_id":"B003","role":"The split-root image of transmitting news or poetry supplies a secondary movement from witnessed event to narrated event."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Victory becomes publicly visible.","after":"As a contained split-root afterimage, victory also becomes transmissible narrative: an arrival that can be carried beyond those who directly saw it."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_wound_drainage_and_relapse","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This cross-domain medical analogy is surprising and branch-distant, but it is anchored by a focus branch for matter collected in a wound, the focus aperture, and a context branch for wound relapse. Downstream prose should present it only as an analogy of accumulated injury, release, and aftercare, never as a lexical paraphrase of the ayah.","focus_anchor":"جاء and الفتح can jointly image accumulated matter reaching an opening, while later غفر carries the possibility of a wound's relapse.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ج ي ء","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000281","branch_id":"B006","role":"Collected matter from a wound supplies the image of injury that has accumulated to a point of release."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ن ص ر","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001510","branch_id":"B002","role":"Redress after oppression supplies the social injury for which release would count as relief."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B001","role":"Opening a closure supplies drainage or exposure of what had been trapped."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"غ ف ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001096","branch_id":"B004","role":"Relapse of illness or wound supplies the warning that opening and relief do not eliminate the need for aftercare."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Victory cleanly removes an external obstacle.","after":"As a contained wound analogy, redress opens and releases accumulated injury, while the command that follows guards against relapse after the dramatic relief."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_opening_as_cutover","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it activates the non-dominant ت ب ب split under توابا, especially its cutting image. It remains anchored to الفتح as an opening beginning and to the packet's supplied split, but downstream prose should call it a cutover shadow of return, not import cutting into the direct meaning of تواب.","focus_anchor":"The opening can be a beginning precisely because a prior configuration is severed at the threshold.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ج ي ء","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000281","branch_id":"B001","role":"Arrival supplies the threshold at which the transition takes effect."},{"source_ref":"110:1","root":"ف ت ح","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001124","branch_id":"B002","role":"An opening beginning supplies the new phase initiated at the threshold."},{"source_ref":"110:3","root":"ت و ب","source_word_indices":["7"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000172","branch_id":"B004","role":"The split-root cutting image supplies a contained severance of the prior order that makes return a genuine reorientation."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Opening simply adds a victorious new condition.","after":"As a contained split-root shadow, the opening is a cutover: it begins a new orientation by severing continuity with the configuration that preceded it."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The arrival-and-opening threshold becomes publicly legible through grouped human entry rather than remaining an invisible transfer of power.","Conquest is revised into permeability: people cross into obedience, while visible entry and inward entrustment remain distinguishable.","The repeated divine name makes aid's source and the entered religion's destination form an anti-appropriation circuit.","The response in 110:3 turns opening from a terminal triumph into the start of praise, protection, nurture, and repeated return.","Juridical, inward, and hydraulic mechanisms remain live beside the prevailing-aid reading rather than being merged into it."],"what_was_special":"The focus-only branch constellations already supported legal redress, gifted disclosure, and a coherent water network. Context then made opening social and porous, exposed an ego-risk in public success, and extended the water network into a carefully contained social-irrigation model. Non-dominant split roots were retained as explicit exploratory activators rather than silently collapsed.","remaining_uncertainties":["The conjunction of aid and opening supports coupling but does not by itself prove that aid causally precedes opening.","The packet leaves outward group entry, inward assent, compelled compliance, and performative display simultaneously possible.","The civic, hydraulic, wound, narration, and cutover models depend on branch constellations or split mappings and should remain analogical rather than direct glosses.","The exact temporal force contributed by كان to the contrast between punctual arrival and enduring return remains open."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Help and opening as an arrival-threshold that creates access.** Lexical evidence: `ج ي ء` B001, coming/arrival; `ج ي ء` B004, bringing or presenting something; `ن ص ر` B001, aid that makes someone prevail; `ف ت ح` B001, opening what is closed; `ف ت ح` B006, key/means of access; `د خ ل` B001, entering. Structural cue: 110:1 says the help/opening \"comes\"; 110:2 immediately shows people entering; 110:3 gives the required response once the threshold has opened. Assigned role: coming marks event-arrival, help supplies efficacy, opening supplies aperture, and entry verifies that the aperture works. Abductive move: I infer that the later entry is the functional test of the earlier opening. Reading change: the ayah reads less as an abstract announcement of victory and more as the moment when a closed boundary becomes passable.\n\n2. **Victory as flowing relief, not only domination.** Lexical evidence: `ن ص ر` B004, rain and relief by rain; `ن ص ر` B007, tributary watercourse; `ف ت ح` B005, water released from its opening; `ج ي ء` B002/B003, water-collecting hollow; `س م و` B004, sky/rain; `ر ب ب` B008/B013, cloud and abundant water; `ف و ج` B001, successive groups. Structural cue: the sequence runs from coming help/opening to people entering in waves, and the branch inventories cluster around rain, water release, tributaries, and collected flow. Assigned role: `نصر` becomes relief-rain, `فتح` becomes the opened outlet, `أفواج` becomes the visible wave pattern downstream. Abductive move: I supply a hydrological causal path: sky/rain relief opens channels, and groups move like tributaries into a collection point. Reading change: \"نصر الله والفتح\" is activated as life-giving release and irrigation, not merely as military conquest.\n\n3. **Opening as adjudication and redress.** Lexical evidence: `ن ص ر` B002, redress after oppression; `ف ت ح` B003, settling a closed dispute by judgment; `د ي ن` B002, reckoning/recompense; `غ ف ر` B002, shielding from consequence; `ت و ب` B003, calling someone to repent/return. Structural cue: 110:1 pairs help with opening; 110:2 locates the mass movement in dīn; 110:3 answers with forgiveness and return. Assigned role: help/redress handles the oppressed side; opening/judgment resolves the closed case; dīn/reckoning supplies the juridical domain; istighfār/tawwāb supplies post-judgment mercy. Abductive move: I infer that an opened victory can be a court-like settlement whose end is not punishment alone but return. Reading change: the ayah reads as a decisive divine ruling that opens the way to pardon, not only as the winning of a contest.\n\n4. **The beginning of a final liturgical sequence.** Lexical evidence: `ف ت ح` B002, opening beginning; `ء ل ه` B002, invocation formula; `س ب ح` B001, worship/prayer; `ح م د` B001, praise/thanksgiving. Structural cue: the packet itself begins with basmalah, 110:1 begins the ordinary surah movement with \"when,\" and 110:3 supplies imperatives of worship. Assigned role: `فتح` opens not only territory/access but a ritual sequence: invocation, event, witnessed entry, praise, and forgiveness. Abductive move: I infer that the temporal `إذا` functions as a hinge: when this beginning arrives, a particular worship-response becomes due. Reading change: 110:1 becomes an opening cue for liturgical action, not only a reportable historical condition.","retrospective_surprises_md":"110:2 made the access reading stronger than a simple victory reading: the proof of `فتح` is not possession but people actually entering. 110:3 then corrected the triumphal branch by requiring tasbīḥ and istighfār, which made judicial redress and mercy more salient than domination. The water-relief reading also gained force retrospectively from the repeated pattern of arrival, opening, entry, groups, covering, and return.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s110/full_context_control/reader_s110_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Arrival opens a route rather than merely announcing an endpoint.** `جَآءَ` anchors `ج ي ء B001`, specifically its coming/arrival variant; `نَصْرُ` anchors `ن ص ر B001` (aid that makes another prevail); and `ٱلْفَتْحُ` anchors `ف ت ح B001` (opening a closed or widened aperture). The conditional `إِذَا` makes this arrival the trigger, while `يَدْخُلُونَ` in `110:2` supplies the motion that an aperture enables. Aid is the enabling force and opening is the traversable result; the directional claim that aid produces access is inferred from their sequence and the later entry. The ayah therefore reads less as two synonymous victory terms and more as a causal mechanism: enabling support arrives, a closure gives way, and passage becomes possible.\n\n2. **The human groups are the visible form of a repeated coming.** The second accepted variant within `ج ي ء B001` is prevailing by repeated coming, and `ف و ج B001` in `أَفْوَاجًا` supplies successive human batches. The singular event `جَآءَ` is immediately unpacked as many arrivals through the imperfect plural `يَدْخُلُونَ`. The packet gives singular arrival followed by successive groups; I infer that the latter manifests the former rather than merely following it. Thus the coming of aid is read distributively: its prevalence becomes visible through wave after wave of entrants.\n\n3. **A hydraulic relief mechanism lies beneath the victory vocabulary.** `نَصْرُ` activates `ن ص ر B004` (relieving rain) and `ن ص ر B007` (tributary channels arriving from afar), while `ٱلْفَتْحُ` activates `ف ت ح B005` (water released through an opening). The water-collecting variant of `ج ي ء B002`, together with the later wide-passage image of `ف و ج B002`, makes the roots cohere materially: remote channels bring relief, an outlet opens, and flow spreads through a widened space. The actual ayat supply only arrival, aid, opening, entry, and groups; the water-flow direction is an analogical inference from their accepted branches. On this reading, victory is life-giving release and distribution, not only martial displacement.\n\n4. **Opening is also a verdict that places victory under moral review.** `ن ص ر B002` contributes redress after oppression and `ف ت ح B003` the settling of a closed dispute by judgment. Later, `د ي ن B002` supplies reckoning/recompense, and `ٱسْتَغْفِرْهُ` plus the final repentance clause supplies post-outcome accountability. Redress and judgment assign the event a juridical role; I infer from the consequent command that even a favorable verdict does not exempt its beneficiary from review. The ayah consequently reads as adjudicated redress whose proper sequel is self-audit, not unrestricted triumph.\n\n5. **What comes is a gift that creates praise rather than human credit.** `نَصْرُ` anchors `ن ص ر B005` (granting or gift), reinforced by the genitive `ٱللَّهِ`; `ح م د B001` later names thanksgiving for benefaction, whereas `ح م د B005` exposes the counter-possibility of claiming praise because of a favor. The packet supplies divine gift followed by praise; I infer that the final command regulates ownership of credit. `نَصْرُ ٱللَّهِ` therefore reads as a received good whose arrival creates an obligation of thanks and blocks the beneficiary from converting success into personal merit.","retrospective_surprises_md":"`110:2` supplied the decisive test for the aperture reading: `يَدْخُلُونَ ... أَفْوَاجًا` turns `ف ت ح B001` into usable passage and activates repeated-coming within the variant inventory of `ج ي ء B001`. Its wide-space branch `ف و ج B002` also made the hydraulic reading unexpectedly coherent. `110:3` then corrected every endpoint reading of victory: praise recasts `ن ص ر B005` as gift, while forgiveness and return place the redress/judgment pairing (`ن ص ر B002`; `ف ت ح B003`) under continuing review.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s110/full_context_control/reader_s110_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/110_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":110,"ayah_ref":"110:1","canonical_ayah_ref":"110:1","baseline":{"source_ref":"110:1","text":"Allah'ın yardımı ve zafer geldiğinde,","target_tokens":[["Allah'ın",["110:1:4"]],["yardımı",["110:1:3","110:1:4"]],["ve",["110:1:5"]],["zafer",["110:1:5"]],["geldiğinde",["110:1:1","110:1:2"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"Yardım ve zafer, yalnızca bir sonucun ilanı değil, kapalı bir sınırı geçilebilir hâle getiren geliş ve eşik olarak belirir; 2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
